# proc Debug Information

## Overview
Debug information is implemented via the Linux proc file system, which reflects the current system runtime status in real time. The recorded information can be used for problem diagnosis and analysis.

**File Directory**

/proc/umap

**Information Viewing Methods**

-   On the console, the cat command can be used to view information, e.g., cat /proc/umap/venc; other common file operation commands can also be used, e.g., cp /proc/umap/ ./ -rf, to copy the proc files under umap to the current directory.
-   In applications, the above files can be treated as ordinary read-only files for read operations, e.g., using fopen, fread, etc.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>The following 2 cases should be noted for parameter descriptions:
>-   For parameters with values \{0, 1\}, if the specific value-meaning correspondence is not listed, then 1 indicates affirmative and 0 indicates negative.
>-   For parameters with values \{aaa, bbb, ccc\}, if the specific value-meaning correspondence is not listed, the parameter meaning can be directly determined from the values aaa, bbb, or ccc.

## SYS
**Debug Information**

```
~ # cat /proc/umap/sys_user
[SYS] Version: [V1.0.0.0 B010 Release], Build Time[Sep  6 2019, 11:38:16]
----------------------------------------moudle status--------------------
status
run
----------------------------------------scale coeff info-----------------
range_level   range_value   hor_luma  hor_chroma   ver_luma  ver_chroma
RANGE_0   (    0,  8/64)    LEVEL_0     LEVEL_0    LEVEL_0     LEVEL_0
RANGE_1   [ 8/64, 10/64)    LEVEL_1     LEVEL_1    LEVEL_1     LEVEL_1
RANGE_2   [10/64, 15/64)    LEVEL_2     LEVEL_2    LEVEL_2     LEVEL_2
RANGE_3   [15/64, 19/64)    LEVEL_3     LEVEL_3    LEVEL_3     LEVEL_3
RANGE_4   [19/64, 24/64)    LEVEL_4     LEVEL_4    LEVEL_4     LEVEL_4
RANGE_5   [24/64, 29/64)    LEVEL_5     LEVEL_5    LEVEL_5     LEVEL_5

RANGE_7   [33/64, 35/64)    LEVEL_7     LEVEL_7    LEVEL_7     LEVEL_7
RANGE_8   [35/64, 38/64)    LEVEL_8     LEVEL_8    LEVEL_8     LEVEL_8
RANGE_9   [38/64, 42/64)    LEVEL_9     LEVEL_9    LEVEL_9     LEVEL_9
RANGE_10  [42/64, 45/64)   LEVEL_10    LEVEL_10   LEVEL_10    LEVEL_10
RANGE_11  [45/64, 48/64)   LEVEL_11    LEVEL_11   LEVEL_11    LEVEL_11
RANGE_12  [48/64, 51/64)   LEVEL_12    LEVEL_12   LEVEL_12    LEVEL_12
RANGE_13  [51/64, 53/64)   LEVEL_13    LEVEL_13   LEVEL_13    LEVEL_13
RANGE_14  [53/64, 55/64)   LEVEL_14    LEVEL_14   LEVEL_14    LEVEL_14

**Debug Information Analysis**

Records the current usage of the SYS module.

</td>
</tr>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row385mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p387mcpsimp"><a name="p387mcpsimp"></a><a name="p387mcpsimp"></a>fir_dev</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p389mcpsimp"><a name="p389mcpsimp"></a><a name="p389mcpsimp"></a>Device number of the first level in the binding relationship. Data is sent from the first level to the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row390mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p392mcpsimp"><a name="p392mcpsimp"></a><a name="p392mcpsimp"></a>fir_chn</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p394mcpsimp"><a name="p394mcpsimp"></a><a name="p394mcpsimp"></a>Channel number of the first level in the binding relationship. Data is sent from the first level to the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row395mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p397mcpsimp"><a name="p397mcpsimp"></a><a name="p397mcpsimp"></a>sec_mod</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p399mcpsimp"><a name="p399mcpsimp"></a><a name="p399mcpsimp"></a>Module name of the second level in the binding relationship. Data is sent from the first level to the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row400mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p402mcpsimp"><a name="p402mcpsimp"></a><a name="p402mcpsimp"></a>sec_dev</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p404mcpsimp"><a name="p404mcpsimp"></a><a name="p404mcpsimp"></a>Device number of the second level in the binding relationship. Data is sent from the first level to the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row405mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p407mcpsimp"><a name="p407mcpsimp"></a><a name="p407mcpsimp"></a>sec_chn</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p409mcpsimp"><a name="p409mcpsimp"></a><a name="p409mcpsimp"></a>Channel number of the second level in the binding relationship. Data is sent from the first level to the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row410mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p412mcpsimp"><a name="p412mcpsimp"></a><a name="p412mcpsimp"></a>tir_mod</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p414mcpsimp"><a name="p414mcpsimp"></a><a name="p414mcpsimp"></a>Module name of the third level in the binding relationship. If there is a third-level binding relationship, data is sent from the second level to the third level; otherwise displays as null.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row415mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p417mcpsimp"><a name="p417mcpsimp"></a><a name="p417mcpsimp"></a>tir_dev</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p419mcpsimp"><a name="p419mcpsimp"></a><a name="p419mcpsimp"></a>Device number of the third level in the binding relationship.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row420mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p422mcpsimp"><a name="p422mcpsimp"></a><a name="p422mcpsimp"></a>tir_chn</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p424mcpsimp"><a name="p424mcpsimp"></a><a name="p424mcpsimp"></a>Channel number of the third level in the binding relationship.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row425mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p427mcpsimp"><a name="p427mcpsimp"></a><a name="p427mcpsimp"></a>send_cnt</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p429mcpsimp"><a name="p429mcpsimp"></a><a name="p429mcpsimp"></a>Count of data sent from the first level to the second level (generally in units of frames. The send count from VPSS to VO is always 0, as it is not counted in SYS; check the proc information of each module for the count).</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row430mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p432mcpsimp"><a name="p432mcpsimp"></a><a name="p432mcpsimp"></a>rst_cnt</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p434mcpsimp"><a name="p434mcpsimp"></a><a name="p434mcpsimp"></a>Number of times the first level resets the second level.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

If this count is 0, it indicates that frame loss may have occurred due to insufficient buffer blocks.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row596mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p598mcpsimp"><a name="p598mcpsimp"></a><a name="p598mcpsimp"></a>blk</p>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row601mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p603mcpsimp"><a name="p603mcpsimp"></a><a name="p603mcpsimp"></a>vi/vo/vgs/venc/vdec/h265e/h264e/jpege/jpegd /vpss/dis/user/pciv/ai/aenc/rc/vfmw/gdc/avs/  rect/match/mcf/vda/vpp/uvc/adapt</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p605mcpsimp"><a name="p605mcpsimp"></a><a name="p605mcpsimp"></a>Module name (where adapt represents vdec_adapt)</p>
<p id="p606mcpsimp"><a name="p606mcpsimp"></a><a name="p606mcpsimp"></a>The corresponding number below indicates how many places in the current module occupy this buffer block in the buffer pool.</p>
<a name="ul607mcpsimp"></a><a name="ul607mcpsimp"></a><ul id="ul607mcpsimp"><li>0: Not occupied.</li><li>Non-zero: occupancy count.</li></ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row20222255125217"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p822255520522"><a name="p822255520522"></a><a name="p822255520522"></a>get</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1022219551523"><a name="p1022219551523"></a><a name="p1022219551523"></a>Module name that obtained the buffer block. Indicates which module obtained this buffer block from the buffer pool.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row13826105117526"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p108266510523"><a name="p108266510523"></a><a name="p108266510523"></a>free_bytes</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p20826205113520"><a name="p20826205113520"></a><a name="p20826205113520"></a>Difference between the buffer block size and the actual obtained buffer block size. Used to observe cases where a small buffer block is actually needed but a large buffer block is occupied.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## LOG
**Debug Information**

```
~ # cat /proc/umap/logmpp_user

**Debug Information Analysis**

-   Records the current debug level of each module.
-   cat /proc/umap/logmpp is used to obtain kernel-mode logmpp level information.
-   cat /proc/umap/logmpp\_user is used to obtain user-mode logmpp level information.
-   Modifying logmpp level:
    -   Use the echo command to modify the debug level of a module, e.g.: echo "venc=4" > /proc/umap/logmpp (logmpp\_user for user mode);
    -   Use: echo "all=4" > /proc/umap/logmpp (logmpp\_user for user mode) to modify the debug level of all modules.

-   View logmpp: open using cat /dev/logmpp, exit by pressing Ctrl+c.

**Debug Information Analysis**

<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" id="mcps1.1.4.1.2"><p id="p19255mcpsimp"><a name="p19255mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19255mcpsimp"></a>Description</p>

<tbody><tr id="row97466461048"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="26.979999999999997%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p750214551141"><a name="p750214551141"></a><a name="p750214551141"></a>-</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20.02%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p146454825319"><a name="p146454825319"></a><a name="p146454825319"></a>anony</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="53%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1557451575"><a name="p1557451575"></a><a name="p1557451575"></a>Module parameter, indicates whether to use anonymous MMZ.</p>
<a name="ul198221627192"></a><a name="ul198221627192"></a><ul id="ul198221627192"><li>0: Do not use anonymous MMZ.</li></ul>
<a name="ul114792048585"></a><a name="ul114792048585"></a><ul id="ul114792048585"><li>1: Use anonymous MMZ. (Default value)</li></ul>
<p id="p164184895310"><a name="p164184895310"></a><a name="p164184895310"></a>Note: The current assignment must be 1.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row197999491541"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p33855535573"><a name="p33855535573"></a><a name="p33855535573"></a>mmz_allocator</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1017819469101"><a name="p1017819469101"></a><a name="p1017819469101"></a>Module parameter, indicates the MMZ memory allocator type.</p>
<a name="ul9781191914118"></a><a name="ul9781191914118"></a><ul id="ul9781191914118"><li>ot: Use the default allocator. (Default value)</li><li>cma: Use the CMA-based allocator.</li></ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1471910431543"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p2670114345315"><a name="p2670114345315"></a><a name="p2670114345315"></a>mem_process_isolation</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p10670184385318"><a name="p10670184385318"></a><a name="p10670184385318"></a>Module parameter, indicates whether to enable the process isolation attribute for mmz buffers.</p>
<a name="ul850751641413"></a><a name="ul850751641413"></a><ul id="ul850751641413"><li>0: Disable the process isolation attribute for mmz buffers.</li></ul>
<a name="ul9507916201413"></a><a name="ul9507916201413"></a><ul id="ul9507916201413"><li>1: Enable the process isolation attribute for mmz buffers. (Default value)</li></ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19257mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="4" valign="top" width="26.979999999999997%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p19259mcpsimp"><a name="p19259mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19259mcpsimp"></a>ZONE</p>
</td>

</td>

<tr id="row19264mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p19266mcpsimp"><a name="p19266mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19266mcpsimp"></a>GFP</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p96017914191"><a name="p96017914191"></a><a name="p96017914191"></a>Indicates the allocation pool attribute, mainly used to specify which memory type the MMZ is located on for boards with multiple memory types (e.g., DDR, SDRAM, DDR2, DDR3). 0 means automatic; currently this value is generally set to 0.</p>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p118953595461"><a name="p118953595461"></a><a name="p118953595461"></a>Starting virtual address of the MMB</p>

<tr id="row19782549131912"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p978234971916"><a name="p978234971916"></a><a name="p978234971916"></a>flags</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p15782204912191"><a name="p15782204912191"></a><a name="p15782204912191"></a>Current MMB flag status</p>
<p id="p1637833415286"><a name="p1637833415286"></a><a name="p1637833415286"></a>bit[0]: Indicates whether the kernel virtual address is mapped. Value 1 means mapped, value 0 means not mapped.</p>
<p id="p86973224313"><a name="p86973224313"></a><a name="p86973224313"></a>bit[1]: Indicates whether the mapped kernel virtual address has cache attribute. Value 1 means cacheable, value 0 means non-cacheable.</p>
<p id="p751565183419"><a name="p751565183419"></a><a name="p751565183419"></a>bit[2]: Indicates whether the MMZ has been released. Value 1 means released, value 0 means occupied.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row991616915208"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p791718972014"><a name="p791718972014"></a><a name="p791718972014"></a>kernel_only</p>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p139178917204"><a name="p139178917204"></a><a name="p139178917204"></a>Whether the MMB is kernel-only. Value 1 means kernel-only, value 0 means both kernel and user mode can use it.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row10599112720714"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p2038818319216"><a name="p2038818319216"></a><a name="p2038818319216"></a>share_all</p>

<tr id="row61241435213"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1412574302113"><a name="p1412574302113"></a><a name="p1412574302113"></a>block_number</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p212524316217"><a name="p212524316217"></a><a name="p212524316217"></a>Total number of MMBs</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

-   The anony, mmz\_allocator, and mem\_process\_isolation parameters are only displayed under Linux systems.
-   The share\_all and pid information in MMB is only valid under Linux systems.

**Debug Information Analysis**

Records the current status debug information of the channel scheduler module.

**Debug Information Analysis**

Records recently completed tasks of the VGS module, the task with the highest recent latency, historical cumulative information, and interrupt-related information.

**Parameter Description**

<a name="table959mcpsimp"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row965mcpsimp"><th class="cellrowborder" colspan="2" valign="top" id="mcps1.1.4.1.1"><p id="p967mcpsimp"><a name="p967mcpsimp"></a><a name="p967mcpsimp"></a>Parameter</p>
</th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" id="mcps1.1.4.1.2"><p id="p969mcpsimp"><a name="p969mcpsimp"></a><a name="p969mcpsimp"></a>Description</p>
</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="row971mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="3" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p xml:lang="pt-BR" id="p973mcpsimp"><a name="p973mcpsimp"></a><a name="p973mcpsimp"></a>module param</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p975mcpsimp"><a name="p975mcpsimp"></a><a name="p975mcpsimp"></a>g_max_job_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p977mcpsimp"><a name="p977mcpsimp"></a><a name="p977mcpsimp"></a>Maximum number of jobs. Linux defaults to 128.</p>
<p id="p978mcpsimp"><a name="p978mcpsimp"></a><a name="p978mcpsimp"></a>This value only needs to be specified when inserting the module if the VGS-supported job count needs to be adjusted.</p>
<p id="p7299022103711"><a name="p7299022103711"></a><a name="p7299022103711"></a>Valid range: [20, 400]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row979mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p981mcpsimp"><a name="p981mcpsimp"></a><a name="p981mcpsimp"></a>g_max_task_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p983mcpsimp"><a name="p983mcpsimp"></a><a name="p983mcpsimp"></a>Maximum number of tasks. Linux defaults to 200.</p>
<p id="p984mcpsimp"><a name="p984mcpsimp"></a><a name="p984mcpsimp"></a>This value only needs to be specified when inserting the module if the VGS-supported task count needs to be adjusted.</p>
<p id="p495187133818"><a name="p495187133818"></a><a name="p495187133818"></a>Valid range: [20, 800]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row985mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p987mcpsimp"><a name="p987mcpsimp"></a><a name="p987mcpsimp"></a>g_max_node_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p989mcpsimp"><a name="p989mcpsimp"></a><a name="p989mcpsimp"></a>Maximum number of nodes. Linux defaults to 200.</p>
<p id="p990mcpsimp"><a name="p990mcpsimp"></a><a name="p990mcpsimp"></a>This value only needs to be specified when inserting the module if the VGS-supported node count needs to be adjusted.</p>
<p id="p1366512199387"><a name="p1366512199387"></a><a name="p1366512199387"></a>Valid range: [20, 800]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row991mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="10" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p993mcpsimp"><a name="p993mcpsimp"></a><a name="p993mcpsimp"></a>recent job info1</p>
<p id="p994mcpsimp"><a name="p994mcpsimp"></a><a name="p994mcpsimp"></a>Information of recently completed jobs</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p996mcpsimp"><a name="p996mcpsimp"></a><a name="p996mcpsimp"></a>seq_no</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p998mcpsimp"><a name="p998mcpsimp"></a><a name="p998mcpsimp"></a>Print sequence number.</p>
<p id="p999mcpsimp"><a name="p999mcpsimp"></a><a name="p999mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: [0, 7]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1000mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1002mcpsimp"><a name="p1002mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1002mcpsimp"></a>mod_name</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1004mcpsimp"><a name="p1004mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1004mcpsimp"></a>Module name that submitted this job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1005mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1007mcpsimp"><a name="p1007mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1007mcpsimp"></a>job_hdl</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1009mcpsimp"><a name="p1009mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1009mcpsimp"></a>Handle number of this job, for debugging use.</p>
<p id="p1010mcpsimp"><a name="p1010mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1010mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: [0, max_job_num-1]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1011mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1013mcpsimp"><a name="p1013mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1013mcpsimp"></a>task_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1015mcpsimp"><a name="p1015mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1015mcpsimp"></a>Number of tasks contained in this job.</p>
<p id="p1016mcpsimp"><a name="p1016mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1016mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: [0, max_task_num-1]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1017mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1019mcpsimp"><a name="p1019mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1019mcpsimp"></a>state</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1021mcpsimp"><a name="p1021mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1021mcpsimp"></a>Processing status of this job.</p>
<p id="p1022mcpsimp"><a name="p1022mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1022mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: {free, begin, endjob, submit, wait_proc, procing, proced, proc_err}</p>
<a name="ul1023mcpsimp"></a><a name="ul1023mcpsimp"></a><ul id="ul1023mcpsimp"><li>free: indicates the job is in idle state;</li><li>begin: indicates the job was created successfully;</li><li>endjob: indicates the job was submitted successfully;</li><li>submit: indicates the job was submitted to hardware successfully;</li><li>wait_proc: indicates the job is waiting for hardware processing</li><li>procing: indicates the job is being processed;</li><li>proced: indicates the job was processed successfully;</li><li>proc_err: indicates job processing failure.</li></ul>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1032mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1034mcpsimp"><a name="p1034mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1034mcpsimp"></a>in_size</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1036mcpsimp"><a name="p1036mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1036mcpsimp"></a>Sum of input image areas of all tasks under this job.</p>
<p id="p1037mcpsimp"><a name="p1037mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1037mcpsimp"></a>Unit: pixels.</p>
<p id="p1038mcpsimp"><a name="p1038mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1038mcpsimp"></a>Each time a task is added to this job, this item adds the input area of that task.</p>
<p id="p1039mcpsimp"><a name="p1039mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1039mcpsimp"></a>VGS scaling performance is mainly affected by the image input and output areas. Generally, the larger the sum of the job's input and output image areas (in_size, out_size), the longer the processing time (cost_time) for the task.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1040mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1042mcpsimp"><a name="p1042mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1042mcpsimp"></a>out_size</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1044mcpsimp"><a name="p1044mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1044mcpsimp"></a>Sum of output image areas of all tasks under this job.</p>
<p id="p1045mcpsimp"><a name="p1045mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1045mcpsimp"></a>Unit: pixels.</p>
<p id="p1046mcpsimp"><a name="p1046mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1046mcpsimp"></a>Each time a task is added to this job, this item adds the output area of that task.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1047mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1049mcpsimp"><a name="p1049mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1049mcpsimp"></a>cost_time</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1051mcpsimp"><a name="p1051mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1051mcpsimp"></a>Duration from job submission to completion.</p>
<p id="p1052mcpsimp"><a name="p1052mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1052mcpsimp"></a>Unit: us.</p>
<p id="p1053mcpsimp"><a name="p1053mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1053mcpsimp"></a>This time includes the software, hardware, and interrupt service routine processing time for this task.</p>
<p id="p1054mcpsimp"><a name="p1054mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1054mcpsimp"></a>If VGS performance is insufficient (e.g., too many scaling tasks submitted, exceeding performance limits), this value may exceed expectations.</p>
<p id="p1055mcpsimp"><a name="p1055mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1055mcpsimp"></a>For example, in a PAL preview scenario, if the completion time of a job exceeds 40ms, it will cause preview image stuttering. In this case, check whether the workload exceeds VGS performance.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1056mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1058mcpsimp"><a name="p1058mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1058mcpsimp"></a>hw_time</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1060mcpsimp"><a name="p1060mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1060mcpsimp"></a>Duration of hardware processing time for this job.</p>
<p id="p1061mcpsimp"><a name="p1061mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1061mcpsimp"></a>Unit: us.</p>
<p id="p1062mcpsimp"><a name="p1062mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1062mcpsimp"></a>This time is the interval from submitting to the hardware until the interrupt is reported.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1063mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1065mcpsimp"><a name="p1065mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1065mcpsimp"></a>vgs</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1067mcpsimp"><a name="p1067mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1067mcpsimp"></a>Which hardware unit this job is submitted to for processing.</p>
<p id="p1068mcpsimp"><a name="p1068mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1068mcpsimp"></a>Note:SS626V100 , Numbers preceded by“*”Symbol indicatessubmittedToVPSSprocessing，May be multipleIP，e.g.,“*01”Is thensubmittedToVPSS0andVPSS1.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1069mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1071mcpsimp"><a name="p1071mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1071mcpsimp"></a>max waste time job info</p>
<p id="p1072mcpsimp"><a name="p1072mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1072mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the highest recent latency</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1074mcpsimp"><a name="p1074mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1074mcpsimp"></a>All fields same as RECENT</p>
<p id="p1075mcpsimp"><a name="p1075mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1075mcpsimp"></a>fields of JOB INFO</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1077mcpsimp"><a name="p1077mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1077mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the longest latency among the last 500 tasks.</p>
<p id="p1078mcpsimp"><a name="p1078mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1078mcpsimp"></a>All fields are the same as the recent job info fields; see above for specific meanings.</p>
<p id="p1079mcpsimp"><a name="p1079mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1079mcpsimp"></a>This set of values is updated when a task with longer latency appears or the total number of tasks exceeds 500.</p>
<p id="p1080mcpsimp"><a name="p1080mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1080mcpsimp"></a>From this set of values, you can understand the recent VGS running performance and whether VGS processing delays have occurred.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1081mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="18" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1083mcpsimp"><a name="p1083mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1083mcpsimp"></a>recent job info2</p>
<p id="p1084mcpsimp"><a name="p1084mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1084mcpsimp"></a>Information of recently completed jobs</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1086mcpsimp"><a name="p1086mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1086mcpsimp"></a>seq_no</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1088mcpsimp"><a name="p1088mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1088mcpsimp"></a>Print sequence number.</p>
<p id="p1089mcpsimp"><a name="p1089mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1089mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: [0, 7]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1090mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1092mcpsimp"><a name="p1092mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1092mcpsimp"></a>crop</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1094mcpsimp"><a name="p1094mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1094mcpsimp"></a>CROP enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1095mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1097mcpsimp"><a name="p1097mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1097mcpsimp"></a>cover</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1099mcpsimp"><a name="p1099mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1099mcpsimp"></a>Cover enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1100mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1102mcpsimp"><a name="p1102mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1102mcpsimp"></a>mosaic</p>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1105mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1107mcpsimp"><a name="p1107mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1107mcpsimp"></a>osd</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1110mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1112mcpsimp"><a name="p1112mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1112mcpsimp"></a>zme</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1114mcpsimp"><a name="p1114mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1114mcpsimp"></a>Scaling enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1115mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1117mcpsimp"><a name="p1117mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1117mcpsimp"></a>lba</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1119mcpsimp"><a name="p1119mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1119mcpsimp"></a>LBA enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1120mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1122mcpsimp"><a name="p1122mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1122mcpsimp"></a>luma</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1124mcpsimp"><a name="p1124mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1124mcpsimp"></a>Luma and statistics enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>

</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1130mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1132mcpsimp"><a name="p1132mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1132mcpsimp"></a>copy</p>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1135mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1137mcpsimp"><a name="p1137mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1137mcpsimp"></a>rotate</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1139mcpsimp"><a name="p1139mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1139mcpsimp"></a>Rotation enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1140mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1142mcpsimp"><a name="p1142mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1142mcpsimp"></a>bd</p>

<tr id="row1145mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1147mcpsimp"><a name="p1147mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1147mcpsimp"></a>bypass</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1150mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1152mcpsimp"><a name="p1152mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1152mcpsimp"></a>corner</p>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1155mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1157mcpsimp"><a name="p1157mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1157mcpsimp"></a>fpd</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1159mcpsimp"><a name="p1159mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1159mcpsimp"></a>Fpd enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1160mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1162mcpsimp"><a name="p1162mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1162mcpsimp"></a>gme</p>
</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1165mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1167mcpsimp"><a name="p1167mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1167mcpsimp"></a>stitch</p>
</td>

</tr>
<tr id="row1170mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1172mcpsimp"><a name="p1172mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1172mcpsimp"></a>low_delay</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1174mcpsimp"><a name="p1174mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1174mcpsimp"></a>Low-latency output enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1175mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1177mcpsimp"><a name="p1177mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1177mcpsimp"></a>max waste time job info2</p>
<p id="p1178mcpsimp"><a name="p1178mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1178mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the highest recent latency</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1180mcpsimp"><a name="p1180mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1180mcpsimp"></a>All fields same as recent job info 2</p>
<p id="p1181mcpsimp"><a name="p1181mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1181mcpsimp"></a>Members of</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1183mcpsimp"><a name="p1183mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1183mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the longest latency among the last 500 tasks.</p>
<p id="p1184mcpsimp"><a name="p1184mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1184mcpsimp"></a>All fields are the same as the recent job info fields; see above for specific meanings.</p>
<p id="p1185mcpsimp"><a name="p1185mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1185mcpsimp"></a>This set of values is updated when a task with longer latency appears or the total number of tasks exceeds 500.</p>
<p id="p1186mcpsimp"><a name="p1186mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1186mcpsimp"></a>From this set of values, you can understand recentVGSrunningperformance，andwhetherHas occurredVGSprocessingProcessing delays</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1187mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="6" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1189mcpsimp"><a name="p1189mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1189mcpsimp"></a>recent job info3</p>
<p id="p1190mcpsimp"><a name="p1190mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1190mcpsimp"></a>Information of recently completed jobs</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1192mcpsimp"><a name="p1192mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1192mcpsimp"></a>seq_no</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1194mcpsimp"><a name="p1194mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1194mcpsimp"></a>Print sequence number.</p>
<p id="p1195mcpsimp"><a name="p1195mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1195mcpsimp"></a>Valid range: [0, 7]</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1196mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1198mcpsimp"><a name="p1198mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1198mcpsimp"></a>video_format</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1200mcpsimp"><a name="p1200mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1200mcpsimp"></a>Input/output video format transformation of the last task in this job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1201mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1203mcpsimp"><a name="p1203mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1203mcpsimp"></a>pixel_format</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1205mcpsimp"><a name="p1205mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1205mcpsimp"></a>Input/output pixel format transformation of the last task in this job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1206mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1208mcpsimp"><a name="p1208mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1208mcpsimp"></a>cmp_mode</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1210mcpsimp"><a name="p1210mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1210mcpsimp"></a>Input/output compression mode transformation of the last task in this job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1211mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1213mcpsimp"><a name="p1213mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1213mcpsimp"></a>mirror</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1215mcpsimp"><a name="p1215mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1215mcpsimp"></a>mirror enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1216mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1218mcpsimp"><a name="p1218mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1218mcpsimp"></a>flip</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1220mcpsimp"><a name="p1220mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1220mcpsimp"></a>flip enabled (0: disabled, 1: enabled)</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1221mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1223mcpsimp"><a name="p1223mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1223mcpsimp"></a>max waste time job info3</p>
<p id="p1224mcpsimp"><a name="p1224mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1224mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the highest recent latency</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1226mcpsimp"><a name="p1226mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1226mcpsimp"></a>All fields same as recent job info 3</p>
<p id="p1227mcpsimp"><a name="p1227mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1227mcpsimp"></a>Members of</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1229mcpsimp"><a name="p1229mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1229mcpsimp"></a>Information of the job with the longest latency among the last 500 tasks.</p>
<p id="p1230mcpsimp"><a name="p1230mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1230mcpsimp"></a>All fields are the same as the recent job info fields; see above for specific meanings.</p>
<p id="p1231mcpsimp"><a name="p1231mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1231mcpsimp"></a>This set of values is updated when a task with longer latency appears or the total number of tasks exceeds 500.</p>
<p id="p1232mcpsimp"><a name="p1232mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1232mcpsimp"></a>From this set of values, you can understand recentVGSrunningperformance，andwhetherHas occurredVGSprocessingProcessing delays</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1233mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="8" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1235mcpsimp"><a name="p1235mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1235mcpsimp"></a>vgs job status</p>
<p id="p1236mcpsimp"><a name="p1236mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1236mcpsimp"></a>VGS job status</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1238mcpsimp"><a name="p1238mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1238mcpsimp"></a>success</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1240mcpsimp"><a name="p1240mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1240mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of successfully processed jobs.</p>
<p id="p1241mcpsimp"><a name="p1241mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1241mcpsimp"></a>Incremented by 1 when hardware processing succeeds.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1242mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1244mcpsimp"><a name="p1244mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1244mcpsimp"></a>fail</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1246mcpsimp"><a name="p1246mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1246mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of jobs that failed processing.</p>
<p id="p1247mcpsimp"><a name="p1247mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1247mcpsimp"></a>Incremented by 1 when VGS submits a task to the driver layer and it fails. When this value increases, the failure reason can be found by checking the logs.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1248mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1250mcpsimp"><a name="p1250mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1250mcpsimp"></a>cancel</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1252mcpsimp"><a name="p1252mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1252mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of actively canceled jobs, including those canceled by internal module calls.</p>
<p id="p1253mcpsimp"><a name="p1253mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1253mcpsimp"></a>Incremented by 1 when the user calls the cancelJob interface; if adding a task to a job fails, the user will cancel the job. When this value increases, the failure reason can be found by checking the logs.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1254mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1256mcpsimp"><a name="p1256mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1256mcpsimp"></a>all_job_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1258mcpsimp"><a name="p1258mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1258mcpsimp"></a>Total number of VGS job nodes, same as the max_job_num value, generally 128.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1259mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1261mcpsimp"><a name="p1261mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1261mcpsimp"></a>free_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1263mcpsimp"><a name="p1263mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1263mcpsimp"></a>Number of free job nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1264mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1266mcpsimp"><a name="p1266mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1266mcpsimp"></a>begin_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1268mcpsimp"><a name="p1268mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1268mcpsimp"></a>Number of jobs created by the user but not yet submitted (EndJob interface).</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1269mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p xml:lang="fr-FR" id="p1271mcpsimp"><a name="p1271mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1271mcpsimp"></a>busy_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1273mcpsimp"><a name="p1273mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1273mcpsimp"></a>Number of tasks submitted by the user (EndJob interface) but not yet submitted to hardware for processing.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1274mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p xml:lang="fr-FR" id="p1276mcpsimp"><a name="p1276mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1276mcpsimp"></a>procing_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1278mcpsimp"><a name="p1278mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1278mcpsimp"></a>Number of tasks currently being processed by hardware.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1279mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="6" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1281mcpsimp"><a name="p1281mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1281mcpsimp"></a>vgs task status</p>
<p id="p1282mcpsimp"><a name="p1282mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1282mcpsimp"></a>VGS task status</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1284mcpsimp"><a name="p1284mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1284mcpsimp"></a>success</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1286mcpsimp"><a name="p1286mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1286mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of successfully processed tasks.</p>
<p id="p1287mcpsimp"><a name="p1287mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1287mcpsimp"></a>A job contains 1 or more tasks. When a job succeeds, it means all its tasks have succeeded, so this value accumulates faster than the job's success item.</p>
<p id="p1288mcpsimp"><a name="p1288mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1288mcpsimp"></a>When hardware processes a job successfully, this value accumulates the number of tasks under that job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1289mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1291mcpsimp"><a name="p1291mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1291mcpsimp"></a>fail</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1293mcpsimp"><a name="p1293mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1293mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of tasks that failed processing.</p>
<p id="p1294mcpsimp"><a name="p1294mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1294mcpsimp"></a>A job failure will cause failure of all tasks under it. When a job execution fails, this item accumulates the number of failed tasks under it.</p>
<p id="p1295mcpsimp"><a name="p1295mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1295mcpsimp"></a>When this value increases, the failure reason can be found by checking the logs.failedReason.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1296mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1298mcpsimp"><a name="p1298mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1298mcpsimp"></a>cancel</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1300mcpsimp"><a name="p1300mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1300mcpsimp"></a>Cumulative number of actively canceled tasks, including those canceled by internal module calls.</p>
<p id="p1301mcpsimp"><a name="p1301mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1301mcpsimp"></a>When the user calls the cancelJob interface, i.e., cancels the execution of all tasks under the job, this item accumulates.</p>
<p id="p1302mcpsimp"><a name="p1302mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1302mcpsimp"></a>When this value increases, the failure reason can be found by checking the logs.failedReason.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1303mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1305mcpsimp"><a name="p1305mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1305mcpsimp"></a>all_task_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1307mcpsimp"><a name="p1307mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1307mcpsimp"></a>Total number of VGS task nodes, generally 200.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1308mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1310mcpsimp"><a name="p1310mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1310mcpsimp"></a>free_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1312mcpsimp"><a name="p1312mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1312mcpsimp"></a>Number of free task nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1313mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1315mcpsimp"><a name="p1315mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1315mcpsimp"></a>busy_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1317mcpsimp"><a name="p1317mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1317mcpsimp"></a>Number of tasks added to jobs.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1318mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="7" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1320mcpsimp"><a name="p1320mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1320mcpsimp"></a>vgs node status</p>
<p id="p1321mcpsimp"><a name="p1321mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1321mcpsimp"></a>VGS node information</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1323mcpsimp"><a name="p1323mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1323mcpsimp"></a>all_node_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1325mcpsimp"><a name="p1325mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1325mcpsimp"></a>Total number of task nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1326mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1328mcpsimp"><a name="p1328mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1328mcpsimp"></a>busy_num</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1330mcpsimp"><a name="p1330mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1330mcpsimp"></a>Number of occupied nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1331mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1333mcpsimp"><a name="p1333mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1333mcpsimp"></a>min_free</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1335mcpsimp"><a name="p1335mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1335mcpsimp"></a>Minimum number of free nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1336mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1338mcpsimp"><a name="p1338mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1338mcpsimp"></a>max_in_job</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1340mcpsimp"><a name="p1340mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1340mcpsimp"></a>Maximum number of nodes in a single job.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1341mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1343mcpsimp"><a name="p1343mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1343mcpsimp"></a>submit_fail</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1345mcpsimp"><a name="p1345mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1345mcpsimp"></a>Temporary task submission failure count due to insufficient nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1346mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1348mcpsimp"><a name="p1348mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1348mcpsimp"></a>int_fail</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1350mcpsimp"><a name="p1350mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1350mcpsimp"></a>Task submission failure count in interrupt due to insufficient nodes.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1351mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1353mcpsimp"><a name="p1353mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1353mcpsimp"></a>load_ratio_x</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1355mcpsimp"><a name="p1355mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1355mcpsimp"></a>Load ratio (duty cycle) of the IP numbered x.</p>
<p id="p1356mcpsimp"><a name="p1356mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1356mcpsimp"></a>Note: Statistics are refreshed internally every 1 second.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1357mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" rowspan="8" valign="top" width="16%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1359mcpsimp"><a name="p1359mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1359mcpsimp"></a>vgs drv work status</p>
<p id="p1360mcpsimp"><a name="p1360mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1360mcpsimp"></a>VGS hardware working status information</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="20%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1362mcpsimp"><a name="p1362mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1362mcpsimp"></a>link_int</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="64%" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.2 "><p id="p1364mcpsimp"><a name="p1364mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1364mcpsimp"></a>Chain interrupt count.</p>
<p id="p1365mcpsimp"><a name="p1365mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1365mcpsimp"></a>Note: On SS626V100, this count includes the VPSS chain interrupt count when VGS calls VPSS.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1366mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1368mcpsimp"><a name="p1368mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1368mcpsimp"></a>err_int</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1370mcpsimp"><a name="p1370mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1370mcpsimp"></a>Abnormal interrupt count.</p>
<p id="p1371mcpsimp"><a name="p1371mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1371mcpsimp"></a>Note: On SS626V100, this count includes the VPSS abnormal interrupt count when VGS calls VPSS.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1372mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1374mcpsimp"><a name="p1374mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1374mcpsimp"></a>start_suc0</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1376mcpsimp"><a name="p1376mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1376mcpsimp"></a>VGS0 start success count.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1377mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1379mcpsimp"><a name="p1379mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1379mcpsimp"></a>start_err0</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1381mcpsimp"><a name="p1381mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1381mcpsimp"></a>VGS0 start failure count.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1382mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1384mcpsimp"><a name="p1384mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1384mcpsimp"></a>node_err0</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1386mcpsimp"><a name="p1386mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1386mcpsimp"></a>VGS0 NodeId read error count.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1387mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1389mcpsimp"><a name="p1389mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1389mcpsimp"></a>start_suc1</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1391mcpsimp"><a name="p1391mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1391mcpsimp"></a>VGS1 start success count.</p>
<p id="p1392mcpsimp"><a name="p1392mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1392mcpsimp"></a>Note: For single IP, only start_suc0 information is available.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1393mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1395mcpsimp"><a name="p1395mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1395mcpsimp"></a>start_err1</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1397mcpsimp"><a name="p1397mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1397mcpsimp"></a>VGS1 start failure count.</p>
<p id="p1398mcpsimp"><a name="p1398mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1398mcpsimp"></a>Note: For single IP, only start_err0 information is available.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row1399mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" headers="mcps1.1.4.1.1 "><p id="p1401mcpsimp"><a name="p1401mcpsimp"></a><a name="p1401mcpsimp"></a>node_err1</p>
</td>

**Debug Information Analysis**

During multi-channel H.265 encoding, the most common issue is that the upper-layer software does not retrieve the bitstream in time, causing insufficient bitstream buffer and subsequent encoding frame loss. The best way to confirm this situation is to check discard and unread_stream. discard typically indicates the number of frames dropped because the hardware detected insufficient bitstream buffer. unread_stream indicates the number of remaining bitstream frames in the buffer; the larger this number, the more delayed the user is in retrieving the bitstream.

**Debug Information Analysis**

During multi-channelH.264encodingWhen，The most common issue is that the upper-layer softwareobtainedbitstreamNot timely，ResultingbitstreambufferInsufficient，Thus causingencodingframe loss.The best way to confirm this situation is to checkdiscardandunread\_stream.discardUsually indicates due tohardwareDetectedbitstreambufferInsufficient and thus dropped frames.unread\_streamIndicatesbitstreambufferremaining bitstream frames in，ThiscountThe larger the number，the more delayed the user is in retrieving the bitstream.

Records current encoding bitrate control information.
